### 8. 终端命令 · 退出码 0

```
/bin/zsh -c "python3 - <<'EOF'
import json
with open('snapshot.json') as f:
    d=json.load(f)
hist=d['history']
KC=['Busio','Caliste','Canna','Kyeahoo','Yike']
T1=['Doran','Faker','Keria','Oner','Peyz']

def starters(game):
    if 'players' not in game: return None
    blue={p['name'] for p in game['players'] if p['side']=='blue'}
    red={p['name'] for p in game['players'] if p['side']=='red'}
    return blue,red

# KC blue games
kc_blue=[]
t1_red=[]
for g in hist:
    st=starters(g)
    if st is None: continue
    blue,red=st
    if set(KC) <= blue:
        kc_blue.append(g)
    if set(T1) <= red:
        t1_red.append(g)

print(\"KC as blue games:\", len(kc_blue))
for g in kc_blue:
    print(g['series'], g['n'], 'patch', g['patch'], 'win', g.get('blue_win'), 'gd15', g.get('blue_gd15'), 'vs', g['red'])
print()
print(\"T1 as red games:\", len(t1_red))
for g in t1_red:
    print(g['series'], g['n'], 'patch', g['patch'], 'win(blue)', g.get('blue_win'), 'gd15(blue)', g.get('blue_gd15'), 'vs', g['blue'])
EOF"
```

### 10. 终端命令 · 退出码 0

```
/bin/zsh -c "python3 - <<'EOF'
import json
with open('snapshot.json') as f:
    d=json.load(f)
hist=d['history']
KC=['Busio','Caliste','Canna','Kyeahoo','Yike']
T1=['Doran','Faker','Keria','Oner','Peyz']

def team_side(game):
    if 'players' not in game: return None
    blue={p['name'] for p in game['players'] if p['side']=='blue'}
    red={p['name'] for p in game['players'] if p['side']=='red'}
    res={}
    if set(KC) <= blue: res['KC']='blue'
    if set(KC) <= red: res['KC']='red'
    if set(T1) <= blue: res['T1']='blue'
    if set(T1) <= red: res['T1']='red'
    return res

# all games for each team
def record(team):
    wins=0; n=0; gd15s=[]; ahead15=0; ahead_n=0
    for g in hist:
        ts=team_side(g)
        if ts and team in ts:
            n+=1
            side=ts[team]
            blue_win=g.get('blue_win')
            if blue_win is None: continue
            win = blue_win if side=='blue' else (not blue_win)
            wins += 1 if win else 0
            gd=g.get('blue_gd15')
            if gd is not None:
                # blue-minus-red gold
                ahead = (gd>0) if side=='blue' else (gd<0)
                gd15s.append(gd if side=='blue' else -gd)
                ahead15 += 1 if ahead else 0
                ahead_n += 1
    return wins,n,gd15s,ahead15,ahead_n

for t in ['KC','T1']:
    w,n,gs,a,an=record(t)
    print(t, 'record', w, '/', n, 'winrate', round(w/n,3) if n else None)
    print('  gd15 (team minus opp): mean', round(sum(gs)/len(gs),1) if gs else None, 'n=',len(gs))
    print('  ahead15 rate:', a, '/', an, round(a/an,3) if an else None)

# head to head
print('\\nHead to head games:')
for g in hist:
    ts=team_side(g)
    if ts and 'KC' in ts and 'T1' in ts:
        print(g['series'], g['n'], 'patch', g['patch'], 'blue', g['blue'], 'red', g['red'], 'blue_win', g.get('blue_win'), 'blue_gd15', g.get('blue_gd15'))
EOF"
```

  ahead15 rate: 12 / 19 0.632

  ahead15 rate: 3 / 6 0.5

def team_side(game):
    if 'players' not in game: return None
    blue={p['name'] for p in game['players'] if p['side']=='blue'}
    red={p['name'] for p in game['players'] if p['side']=='red'}
    res={}
    if set(KC) <= blue: res['KC']='blue'
    if set(KC) <= red: res['KC']='red'
    if set(T1) <= blue: res['T1']='blue'
    if set(T1) <= red: res['T1']='red'
    return res

print(\"T1 games:\")
for g in hist:
    ts=team_side(g)
    if ts and 'T1' in ts:
        side=ts['T1']
        opp = g['blue'] if side=='red' else g['red']
        win = g['blue_win'] if side=='blue' else (not g['blue_win'])
        gd = g.get('blue_gd15')
        teamgd = gd if side=='blue' else -gd
        print(f\"{g['series'][:40]} n{g['n']} patch{g['patch']} T1={side} vs {opp:20s} win={win} gd15(T1)={teamgd}\")
print()
print(\"KC games:\")
for g in hist:
    ts=team_side(g)
    if ts and 'KC' in ts:
        side=ts['KC']
        opp = g['blue'] if side=='red' else g['red']
        win = g['blue_win'] if side=='blue' else (not g['blue_win'])
        gd = g.get('blue_gd15')
        teamgd = gd if side=='blue' else -gd
        print(f\"{g['series'][:40]} n{g['n']} patch{g['patch']} KC={side} vs {opp:20s} win={win} gd15(KC)={teamgd}\")
EOF"
```

### 15. 终端命令 · 退出码 1

```
/bin/zsh -c "python3 - <<'EOF'
import json, math
with open('snapshot.json') as f:
    d=json.load(f)
hist=d['history']

# Bradley-Terry via logistic regression on team names (blue vs red)
teams=set()
for g in hist:
    if 'blue_win' not in g or g['blue_win'] is None: continue
    teams.add(g['blue']); teams.add(g['red'])
teams=sorted(teams)
idx={t:i for i,t in enumerate(teams)}
n=len(teams)
# features: blue rating - red rating; iterate MLE via gradient ascent
import numpy as np
ratings=np.zeros(n)
games=[(g['blue'],g['red'],1 if g['blue_win'] else 0) for g in hist if 'blue_win' in g and g['blue_win'] is not None]
for it in range(500):
    grad=np.zeros(n)
    for b,r,y in games:
        p=1/(1+math.exp(-(ratings[idx[b]]-ratings[idx[r]])))
        gd=y-p
        grad[idx[b]]+=gd
        grad[idx[r]]-=gd
    ratings+=0.05*grad
    if np.abs(grad).max()<1e-5: break
for t in ['Karmine Corp','T1']:
    print(t, round(ratings[idx[t]],3))
kc=ratings[idx['Karmine Corp']]
t1=ratings[idx['T1']]
p=1/(1+math.exp(-(kc-t1)))
print('BT p_KC_win (neutral):', round(p,3))
print('BT p_T1_win:', round(1-p,3))

# sorted ratings
rr=sorted([(t, ratings[i]) for t,i in idx.items()], key=lambda x:-x[1])
print('top/bottom teams:', rr[:8], rr[-8:])
EOF"
```

/bin/zsh -c "python3 - <<'EOF'
import json, math
with open('snapshot.json') as f:
    d=json.load(f)
hist=d['history']

teams=set()
for g in hist:
    if 'blue_win' not in g or g['blue_win'] is None: continue
    teams.add(g['blue']); teams.add(g['red'])
teams=sorted(teams)
idx={t:i for i,t in enumerate(teams)}
n=len(teams)
ratings=[0.0]*n
side=0.0
games=[(g['blue'],g['red'],1 if g['blue_win'] else 0) for g in hist if 'blue_win' in g and g['blue_win'] is not None]
for it in range(5000):
    grad=[0.0]*n; gs=0.0
    for b,r,y in games:
        z=ratings[idx[b]]-ratings[idx[r]]+side
        p=1/(1+math.exp(-z))
        gd=y-p
        grad[idx[b]]+=gd
        grad[idx[r]]-=gd
        gs+=gd
    mx=max(max(abs(x) for x in grad),abs(gs))
    for i in range(n): ratings[i]+=0.05*grad[i]
    side+=0.05*gs
    if mx<1e-6: break
kc=ratings[idx['Karmine Corp']]
t1=ratings[idx['T1']]
print('side adv (blue):', round(side,3), '-> blue win prob at equal teams', round(1/(1+math.exp(-side)),3))
p=1/(1+math.exp(-(kc-t1+side)))
print('BT+side p_KC_win (KC blue):', round(p,3))
# neutral for reference
pn=1/(1+math.exp(-(kc-t1)))
print('neutral p_KC:', round(pn,3))
print('KC rating',round(kc,3),'T1 rating',round(t1,3))
EOF"

        grad[idx[b]]+=gd; grad[idx[r]]-=gd; gs+=gd
    mx=max(max(abs(x) for x in grad),abs(gs))
    for i in range(n): ratings[i]+=0.05*grad[i]

kc=ratings[idx['Karmine Corp']]; t1=ratings[idx['T1']]

print('KC ahead-rating',round(kc,3),'T1 ahead-rating',round(t1,3))
# KC ahead15 rate vs T1 behind rate

print('T1 opp ahead15 rate:', 3/6)
EOF"

### 31. 终端命令 · 退出码 0

```
/bin/zsh -c "python3 - <<'EOF'
import math
from collections import defaultdict
import json
with open('snapshot.json') as f:
    d=json.load(f)
hist=d['history']
champ=defaultdict(lambda:[0,0])
for g in hist:
    if g['patch']"'!='"'26.20' or 'blue_win' not in g or g['blue_win'] is None: continue
    for b in g.get('bp',[]):
        if b['action']=='pick':
            win=(g['blue_win'] and b['side']=='blue') or ((not g['blue_win']) and b['side']=='red')
            champ[b['champion']][0]+=win; champ[b['champion']][1]+=1
def logit(w,n):
    p=(w+1)/(n+2)  # laplace
    return math.log(p/(1-p))
kc=['Orianna','Pantheon','Ambessa','Rell','Tristana']
t1=['Cassiopeia','Olaf','Nautilus',\"Kai'Sa\",'Trundle']
for team,champs in [('KC',kc),('T1',t1)]:
    vals=[logit(champ[c][0],champ[c][1]) for c in champs]
    print(team,'avg champ logit',round(sum(vals)/len(vals),3),[round(v,2) for v in vals])
kcl=sum(logit(champ[c][0],champ[c][1]) for c in kc)/5
t1l=sum(logit(champ[c][0],champ[c][1]) for c in t1)/5
# combine with team logit from BT (KC blue vs T1 red)
team_logit=math.log(0.258/0.742)
comb=team_logit+(kcl-t1l)
print('team logit',round(team_logit,3),'champ diff',round(kcl-t1l,3),'combined p',round(1/(1+math.exp(-comb)),3))
EOF"
```

import json
from collections import defaultdict
with open('snapshot.json') as f:
    d=json.load(f)

champ=defaultdict(list)
for g in hist:
    if g['patch']"'!='"'26.20' or 'blue_gd15' not in g or g['blue_gd15'] is None: continue
    for b in g.get('bp',[]):
        if b['action']=='pick':
            gd=g['blue_gd15'] if b['side']=='blue' else -g['blue_gd15']
            champ[b['champion']].append(gd)
for c in ['Orianna','Pantheon','Ambessa','Rell','Tristana','Cassiopeia','Olaf','Nautilus',\"Kai'Sa\",'Trundle']:
    vals=champ[c]
    mean=sum(vals)/len(vals) if vals else None
    ahead=sum(1 for v in vals if v>0) if vals else 0
    print(f\"{c:12s} n={len(vals):2d} mean_gd15={round(mean,0) if mean is not None else 'NA':>7} ahead_rate={ahead}/{len(vals) if vals else 0}\")
EOF"

hist=d['history']
KC=['Busio','Caliste','Canna','Kyeahoo','Yike']
T1=['Doran','Faker','Keria','Oner','Peyz']
# aggregate per-starter KDA and win rate
for team,names in [('KC',KC),('T1',T1)]:
    print(team)
    for nm in names:
        k=dsum=0;n=0;wins=0
        for g in hist:
            for p in g.get('players',[]):
                if p['name']==nm:
                    k+=p.get('k',0);dsum+=p.get('d',0);n+=1
                    # win?
                    win=(g.get('blue_win')== (p['side']=='blue'))
                    wins+=1 if win else 0
        if n:
            print(f\"  {nm:8s} games={n:2d} KDA={k/n:.2f}/{dsum/n:.2f}/{0:.2f} winrate={wins/n:.2f}\")
EOF"
```

### 43. 模型消息

````
I've analyzed the matchup and written the forecast to `prediction.json`.

**Reasoning**
- Direct evidence: T1 beat KC in game 1 of this series with a 23–7 kill differential, a strong signal T1 outclasses KC even though KC led gold at 15.
- Team strength: a Bradley-Terry fit over the 96-game history rates T1 well above KC (~0.24 neutral win probability for KC), consistent with T1's stronger schedule and better per-player K/D/A.
- Blue side: adds only a slight edge; KC is blue here, but that's already folded in.
- Draft: champion win rates on 26.20 favor T1's comp (Cassiopeia/Olaf/Nautilus/Kai'Sa/Trundle) slightly over KC's (Orianna/Pantheon/Ambessa/Rell/Tristana).
- Early game: KC tends to get early leads (ahead at 15 in 63% of games, including game 1), but T1's comp and red-side early stats are comparable, so I set the gold-lead probability just above a coin flip.

**Forecast**
```json
{"p_blue_win": 0.23, "p_blue_ahead_15": 0.52}
```

Both values are valid probabilities in `[0,1]` and the file is saved at `./prediction.json`.
````
